## §12.2.1 Packet Loss Handling (Lossy Transports)

| Unit | Kind | Level | Scenario | Lock | Python | Rust |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| RK-12.2.1-001 | behavior | should | untested: Whether a backend conceals loss, which no runner reaches: a scenario feeds PCM frames, with no RTP sequence numbers. roomkit's SIP backend enables concealment by default and hands it to its RTP dependency, tests/voice/backends/test_sip.py::TestPacketLossConcealment::test_default_enabled, ::TestPacketLossConcealment::test_plc_reaches_call_session and ::TestPacketLossConcealment::test_plc_disabled_reaches_call_session; a real provider is proven in roomkit-providers, and roomkit-rs has no voice backend (O1). | | | |
| RK-12.2.1-005 | behavior | must | untested: What any backend implementing PLC must do on its own transport, which no runner reaches: a scenario feeds PCM frames, with no RTP sequence numbers and no jitter buffer. roomkit's SIP backend hands detection and concealment to its RTP dependency, which tests them (tests/voice/backends/test_sip.py::TestPacketLossConcealment::test_plc_reaches_call_session and ::TestPacketLossConcealment::test_plc_disabled_reaches_call_session check the hand-over); a real provider is proven in roomkit-providers, and roomkit-rs has no voice backend (O1). | | | |
| RK-12.2.1-006 | behavior | must | untested: What any backend implementing PLC must do on its own transport, which no runner reaches: a scenario feeds PCM frames, with no RTP timestamps or sequence numbers. roomkit's SIP backend hands detection and concealment to its RTP dependency, which tests them (tests/voice/backends/test_sip.py::TestPacketLossConcealment::test_plc_reaches_call_session and ::TestPacketLossConcealment::test_plc_disabled_reaches_call_session check the hand-over); a real provider is proven in roomkit-providers, and roomkit-rs has no voice backend (O1). | | | |
| RK-12.2.1-007 | behavior | must | untested: What any backend implementing PLC must do on its own transport, which no runner reaches: a scenario feeds PCM frames, with no RFC 4733 events in an RTP stream. roomkit's SIP backend hands detection and concealment to its RTP dependency, which tests them (tests/voice/backends/test_sip.py::TestPacketLossConcealment::test_plc_reaches_call_session and ::TestPacketLossConcealment::test_plc_disabled_reaches_call_session check the hand-over); a real provider is proven in roomkit-providers, and roomkit-rs has no voice backend (O1). | | | |
| RK-12.2.1-008 | behavior | must | untested: What any backend implementing PLC must do on its own transport, which no runner reaches: a scenario feeds PCM frames and loses no packet. roomkit's SIP backend hands detection and concealment to its RTP dependency, which tests them (tests/voice/backends/test_sip.py::TestPacketLossConcealment::test_plc_reaches_call_session and ::TestPacketLossConcealment::test_plc_disabled_reaches_call_session check the hand-over); a real provider is proven in roomkit-providers, and roomkit-rs has no voice backend (O1). | | | |
| RK-12.2.1-009 | behavior | should | untested: What a backend implementing PLC should do with its codec, which no runner reaches: a scenario feeds PCM frames and negotiates no codec. roomkit's SIP backend hands detection and concealment to its RTP dependency, which tests them (tests/voice/backends/test_sip.py::TestPacketLossConcealment::test_plc_reaches_call_session and ::TestPacketLossConcealment::test_plc_disabled_reaches_call_session check the hand-over); a real provider is proven in roomkit-providers, and roomkit-rs has no voice backend (O1). | | | |
| RK-12.2.1-010 | behavior | must | untested: What any backend implementing PLC must do with a codec that has no native concealment, which no runner reaches: a scenario feeds PCM frames and negotiates no codec. roomkit's SIP backend hands detection and concealment to its RTP dependency, which tests them (tests/voice/backends/test_sip.py::TestPacketLossConcealment::test_plc_reaches_call_session and ::TestPacketLossConcealment::test_plc_disabled_reaches_call_session check the hand-over); a real provider is proven in roomkit-providers, and roomkit-rs has no voice backend (O1). | | | |
| RK-12.2.1-011 | behavior | must | untested: What any backend implementing PLC must do on a long burst of loss, which no runner reaches: a scenario feeds PCM frames and loses no packet. roomkit's SIP backend hands detection and concealment to its RTP dependency, which tests them (tests/voice/backends/test_sip.py::TestPacketLossConcealment::test_plc_reaches_call_session and ::TestPacketLossConcealment::test_plc_disabled_reaches_call_session check the hand-over); a real provider is proven in roomkit-providers, and roomkit-rs has no voice backend (O1). | | | |
| RK-12.2.1-012 | behavior | must | untested: What any backend implementing PLC must expose, which no runner reaches: the runner's scripted backend loses no packet. roomkit's SIP backend syncs concealed_frames into its per-session audio stats, tests/voice/backends/test_sip.py::TestPacketLossConcealment::test_concealed_frames_synced_at_cleanup, but on private state, with packets_lost only logged, a suspect (RMK-2024); a real provider is proven in roomkit-providers, and roomkit-rs has no voice backend (O1). | | | |
| RK-12.2.1-013 | behavior | should | untested: What a backend implementing PLC should annotate, which no runner reaches: the runner's scripted backend conceals nothing. No roomkit code sets metadata.concealed, a suspect (RMK-2024), so no test exists; a real provider is proven in roomkit-providers, and roomkit-rs has no voice backend (O1). | | | |

## §12.2.2 Conversation Context for TTS

| Unit | Kind | Level | Scenario | Lock | Python | Rust |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| RK-12.2.2-009 | behavior | must | decided gap: step, RMK-2023: step: voice.open TTS options (S7): a scripted TTS at each context_level; voice.read shows what each received, its own played turns at SELF, both roles' text at TEXT, the audio too at AUDIO. | | | |
| RK-12.2.2-010 | structural | must | untested: A record shape no scenario observes: Python's TTSContext is pinned in tests/public_api_signatures.json (roomkit.TTSContext) and checked by tests/test_public_api.py; roomkit-rs has no TTS provider (O1). | | | |
| RK-12.2.2-011 | structural | must | untested: A record shape no scenario observes: Python's ConversationTurn is pinned in tests/public_api_signatures.json (roomkit.ConversationTurn) and checked by tests/test_public_api.py; roomkit-rs has no TTS provider (O1). | | | |
| RK-12.2.2-012 | behavior | must | decided gap: step, RMK-2023: step: voice.open TTS options (S7): a TTSContextConfig with enabled false, then a small max_turns or max_audio_seconds; voice.read shows no context, then the oldest turns dropped, the oldest audio dropped with its text kept. | | | |
| RK-12.2.2-013 | behavior | must | decided gap: step, RMK-2023: step: voice.open TTS options (S7): a TTS at NONE receives no context; one at AUDIO receives audio only with include_audio, else every audio null. | | | |
| RK-12.2.2-014 | behavior | must | untested: What any TTS provider must do with the context it is passed, which no runner reaches: roomkit hands a frozen TTSContext whose turns are a tuple of frozen ConversationTurns (src/roomkit/voice/tts/context.py), so a provider cannot rebind their fields, and tests/test_voice_tts_context.py::TestPassingRules::test_the_second_call_hears_the_first shows each call its own context; a real provider is proven in roomkit-providers, and roomkit-rs has no TTS provider (O1). | | | |
| RK-12.2.2-015 | behavior | must | decided gap: step, RMK-2023: step: voice.open TTS options (S7): two sessions, one spoken to alone; voice.read shows each session's context holds its own dialogue only. | | | |
| RK-12.2.2-016 | behavior | must | decided gap: writable, RMK-2023: voice.open with two participants, then a text another channel sends with send_event into the room: voice.read's synthesized list holds it once per session; that each call gets its own session's context needs step S7. | | | |
| RK-12.2.2-017 | behavior | must | decided gap: step, RMK-2023: step: voice.open TTS options (S7): a TTS at TEXT, or at AUDIO with include_audio; the next call's context holds the user turn with the transcript an ON_TRANSCRIPTION hook left, and its utterance. | | | |
| RK-12.2.2-018 | behavior | must | decided gap: step, RMK-2023: step: voice.open TTS options (S7): an ON_TRANSCRIPTION hook that redacts the transcript, with include_audio; the user turn in the next context has no audio. | | | |
| RK-12.2.2-019 | behavior | must | decided gap: step, RMK-2023: step: voice.open TTS options (S7): a speech segment the scripted backchannel detector classifies as a backchannel; the next context holds no turn for it. | | | |

| RK-12.10.1-009 | behavior | must | untested: What any conference backend must deliver, which no runner reaches (the runner's scripted SFU is told the publisher): roomkit's mock and contract, tests/conference/test_mock_backend.py::TestPublishing::test_tracks_carry_room_and_publisher and tests/conference/test_backend_abc.py::TestCallbackFanout::test_audio_frames_are_attributable_to_participant_and_room; a real provider is proven in roomkit-providers, and roomkit-rs has no conference (O1). | | | |
